投資建議 買進

公司簡介 
聚陽為亞太地區知名成衣製造商 ： 公司設立
於 1990 年，主要業務包括成衣設計、製造
與銷售，主要原料包括主料 （本布） 及副料
（拉鍊等） ， 以向國內外知名廠商下單為主 。
公司目前主要生產地區包含越南 、 印尼 、 柬
埔寨、中國、孟加拉、薩爾瓦多，2025 年
公司主要客戶營收比重：

主要客戶 ： GAP、Target、 日系客戶 、Dick′s 
Sporting goods、Kohl′s 
主要競爭對手：

葉家宏 bill.yeh@sinopac.com 
本次報告：電話訪談

聚陽 (1477 TT) 
最壞情況已過，接單表現應逐步回升

永豐觀點 
原油價格維持高檔，地緣政治風險仍存，造成品牌商下單信心保守，考量客戶庫存
水位已低，拉貨力道有望逐步回溫，聚陽營運谷底應已過，評價有望逐步好轉，研
究部對聚陽營運看法維持正向。 
 投資評價與建議 
投資建議維持買進，目標價 258 元：受原物料價格高漲及戰爭影響，客戶下單態
度趨於保守，考量目前客戶庫存水位已低，公司最壞情況應已過，隨著戰爭及原物
料等風險因素解除，聚陽客戶下單信心有望逐步恢復， 惟短期客戶仍未進入補庫存
循環 ， 將抑制聚陽獲利成長動能 ， 預估公司2026/2027 稅後 EPS 15.77/17.18 元，
公司過去四年股利配發率皆達 99%以上，以此推估公司 2027 年配息，殖利率應
達 8%以上，具備殖利率保護，目前本益比處於歷史區間近最下緣，考量公司營運
谷底已過，評價應逐步回升，給予本益比15X，目標價258 元，投資建議維持買進。

ESG 評析 
聚陽企業永續評鑑整體為 B+等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 B 等與 A 等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

資料來源：SinoPac+ ESG 評等 (依循 2024 SinoPac+企業永續評鑑方法學) 
註 1：資誠永續發展服務股份有限公司僅於ＥＳＧ評鑑系統方法學建置過程中，就評分指標提供
專業意見，對於評分結果及評估報告內容之完整性及真實性，不負擔保責任，亦不對閱讀或使用
本評估報告之第三方負任何責任。 
註 2：永豐投顧已獲 SASB 授權，於研究中使用 SASB 重大性地圖之一般議題分類及揭露主題。
SinoPac Securities Investment Service licenses and applies the SASB Materiality Map® 
General Issue Categories and Disclosure Topics in our work.

### Page 2

聚陽為亞太地區知名成衣製造商：公司設立於 1990 年，主要業務包括成衣設計、
製造與銷售，主要原料包括主料（本布）及副料（拉鍊等），以向國內外知名廠商
下單為主。公司目前主要生產地區包含越南、印尼、柬埔寨、中國、孟加拉、薩爾
瓦多，2025 年公司主要客戶營收比重：GAP 占 36%，T arget占 18%，日系客戶
占 18%，Dick′s Sporting goods 占 9%，Kohl′s 占 7%，其他占 12%。 
3Q26 獲利預期季增：聚陽 8 月營收 35.6 億元(+18.5% MoM，-5.1% YoY)，符
合預期。聚陽 8M26 出貨量-12% YoY，價持平 YoY，匯率影響+7% YoY。展望
3Q26，受戰爭及通膨因素影響，客戶下單態度趨於保守，提高短交期訂單比重，
聚陽 3Q26 短單比重提升至 15%，出貨量預期年增約中個位數，ASP 受產品組合
影響而預期年減約 3%，研究部預估聚陽 3Q26 營收 96.4 億元，（+18.1％QoQ，
+8.5％ YoY） ，預估毛利率24.03%，（+0.16 pts. QoQ，+2.12 pts. YoY） ，預估
稅後淨利 10.5 億元（+18.1％ QoQ，+8.5％ YoY） ，稅後EPS 4.26 元。 
獲利表現穩健，維持買進投資建議：展望後市，客戶下單仍趨於保守，聚陽 4Q26
短交期訂單比重預期將達 15-20%，預期 4Q26 出貨量將年增低個位數，ASP 因
公司挑單生產及因應原物料報價調漲 ， 預期將年增5-7%， 取決於短交期訂單比重 。
公司計畫於 2027 年持續擴增印尼及孟加拉產能，2027 年聚陽整體產能預期將年
增約 7-8%，若客戶補庫存力道較預期積極，則有望提升至雙位數以上增長。研究
部評估聚陽受到原物料價格高漲及戰爭影響， 客戶下單態度趨於保守， 聚陽短單比
重已由先前約 10%提升至 15%，考量目前客戶庫存水位已低，公司最壞情況應已
過，隨著戰爭及原物料等風險因素解除，聚陽客戶下單信心有望逐步恢復，惟短期
客戶仍未進入補庫存循環，將抑制聚陽獲利成長動能。綜合以上，研究部預估公司
2026 年營收 356.1 億元（+3.4％ YoY），毛利率 25.39%，稅後淨利 38.9 億元
（+7.6％YoY） ，稅後EPS 15.77 元。預估公司 2027 年營收 384.7 億元（+8.0％ 
YoY） ，毛利率25.46%，稅後淨利 42.4 億元 （+9.0％YoY） ，稅後EPS 17.18 元，
公司過去四年股利配發率皆達 99%以上，以此推估公司 2027 年配息，殖利率應
達 8%以上，具備殖利率保護，聚陽目前本益比處於歷史區間近最下緣，考量公司
營運谷底已過，評價應逐步回升，給予本益比 15X，目標價 258 元，投資建議維
持買進。

中砂 (1560 TT)  Trading Buy

先進製程、先進封裝帶動中砂的鑽石碟的需求，投資建議
給 Trading Buy 。  
投資建議 ：先進製程、先進封裝帶動中砂的鑽石碟的需求，中
砂持續擴充鑽石碟的產能，帶動獲利、評價提升，投資建議給
Trading Buy 。  
中砂 2Q26 獲利大幅年增： 中砂 2Q26 營收 24.93 億元，
QoQ+8.70%/YoY+17.87% ；稅後純益 5.23 億元，
QoQ+19.94%/YoY+96.49% ；稅後 EPS 3.46 元。 2Q26 業外利益
包含投資獲利。  
中砂 2Q26 事業群營運狀況： ABU：受惠於傳產機械與工具機砂
輪需求回溫。 DBU ：受惠於 CMP 鑽石碟於 2.0/1.6nm 製程出貨
持續上升， 2nm 鑽石碟營收增加 84%、1.4nm 鑽石碟開始出貨。
SBU ： 12 吋月產能 36 萬片滿載。  
中砂 2026 年各事業群展望： 傳統砂輪事業部 (ABU) ：2026 年將
受惠需求回溫及新砂輪子公司合併綜效，目標營收較 2025 雙位
數以上成長。  
鑽石碟事業部 (DBU) ：先進製程及先進封裝之鑽石碟需求將逐
季持續增加，美系記憶體及 IDM 客戶，中砂將持續增加市占率
及逐季放量。  
晶圓事業部 (SBU) ：2027 年將增加至 12 吋的月產能到 43 萬片，
高階產品組合比重將持續提高，以提升營收及毛利率。  
群益預估中砂 2026 年 /2027 年稅後 EPS 14.52 元 /21.06 元：群
益預估中砂 2026 年 /2027 年營收為 101.87 億元 /135.79 億元，
YoY+25.00%/+33.30% ；稅後純益 21.97 億元 /31.87 億元，
YoY+61.48%/+45.07% ；稅後 EPS 14.52 元 /21.06 元。

### Page 2

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 9 月 23 日

2 群益投顧 
  ESG ：  
E：(Environmental) 。中砂秉持企業經營須兼顧環境保護之理念，持續落實環境
友善管理策略與措施。國際永續趨勢與國家 2050 淨零路徑推動下，中砂透過
執行組織型溫室氣體盤查、產品碳足跡盤查及導入循環經濟專案，循序漸進達
成減碳目標。  
此外，中砂重視氣候變遷伴隨之風險與機會，亦將氣候行動納入公司風險管理
與營運策略，積極研擬因應策略及應變措施；對於水資源、空氣污染、廢棄物
等環境議題，更推行各項管理與減量方案，朝向永續經營之願景邁進。  
S： (Social) 。中砂長期深耕地方社區創生與投入藝文教育與活動，使參與社會
公益成為中砂的企業文化。秉持著「共好：你好、我好、大家好」的經營理念
及懷著「知恩、感恩、報恩」的心，中砂持續與當地縣市政府、學校、社福機
構團體與自身基金會合作，攜手保留珍貴產業文化，提倡書法教育，為當地社
區帶來實質性的協助與回饋，發揮企業正向影響力。  
中砂座落於台灣著名的陶瓷之鄉 — 鶯歌，期望與鄉里共榮，為社區民眾打造友
善的生活環境，因此長期參與社區關懷與愛鄉感恩公益，連續多年贊助新北市
鶯歌區西鶯里、中湖里的敬老活動與銀髮族共餐活動，並致贈年節禮盒及物資
予鄰里長輩。近年中砂更與民間公益培訓單位合作，透過「學生提案，企業協
力」的方式，與公益團體合作推出聯名禮盒、愛心送餐等創新專案。除了達到
支援公益團體的目的外，中砂更將聯名禮盒分送給鶯歌的鄰里長輩，持續傳遞
公益善心。  
G： (Governance) 。企業永續發展的關鍵建基在健全且完善的公司治理架構上，
使得強化董事會監督管理職能成為中砂精進公 司治理的核心目標。除了保障股
東權益外，中砂更積極建構具備應對傳統及氣候變遷、資訊安全等新興風險的
管理機制，透過完整的鑑別、評估及擬定因應措施的流程，降低潛在風險對公
司營運之衝擊。在持續提升營運韌性的同時，也為利害關係人創造更大的價值。
公司簡介：  
中國砂輪股份有限公司成立於 07/1964 ，總部位於鶯歌，主要從事生產及銷售
砂輪、鑽石碟、再生晶圓等研磨工具。  
公司早期產品以應用於石材加工的傳統砂輪為主，後因取得鑽石碟技術的專利
授權及台系大客戶供應鏈在地化策略，以鑽石碟產品切入半導體產業領域。
2020 年，公司再生晶圓事業部竹科廠於竹南科 學園區落成啟用量產，成為全球
前三大再生晶圓供應商。  
再生晶圓主要台灣同業包含辛耘 (3583 TT) 、昇陽半導體 (8028 TT) ；鑽石碟為
3M(MMM US) 、Abrasive Technology 、Asahi(4216 JP) 、Seasol( 韓 )等。中砂大客
戶包含台系晶圓代工廠、美系記憶體廠等。  
中砂自 04/2025 開始認列日本三井金屬礦業集團旗下的兩家公司 MKS 與 MGT，
歸類在傳統砂輪事業部 (ABU) 。  
營收占比：  
傳統砂輪事業部 (ABU) ，毛利率 20~25% 。用於工具機、機械，近期還有包含半
導體 (碳化矽 )的產業。

  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

Company Report 
公司研究報告 
本報告僅供宏遠投顧內部及客戶參考，雖已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場

1 
2383台光電(Elite Material Co.)

報 告 日 期 ：2026/09/21 研 究 員： 方姿婷  (Lesley  Fang ) 
壹、投 資 建 議 
重點摘要：  
⚫ 2Q26 營收 符 合 預 期，毛 利 率 受1H26 漲 價 提 升，預 期 3Q26 毛利
將反應 26 年 6 月 價 格 再 次 調 漲 續 增。 
⚫ AI 伺 服 器 帶 動 高 階 銅 箔 基 板需 求 強 勁，將2026 年 月 產 能 從615
萬 張 上 修 至2027 年底達 930 萬 張 、2028 年 底 月 產 能 達 到1,110
萬張。 2027 年， NVIDIA  Rubin 平 台 將 帶 動CCL M8+ 、M9 材料
出貨攀升， AI ASIC CCL 拉 貨 動 能 呈 現50~60% 高 度 成 長 。 
⚫ 2H26 低軌 S 客戶 V3 版本 新型 火 箭 搭 載 低 軌 衛 星 ， 所 採 用 之 銅
箔 基 板 升 級 至M8 等 級 推 升 材 料 成 本 與PCB 產 值 。

一. 2Q26 營收與財報 符合市場預期， 3Q26 展望正向  
2Q26 台 光 電 營 收472.75 億 元 ，+42.97%QoQ 、+110.03%YoY ， 毛 利
率為 33.85% ， 稅 後 淨 利98.73 億 元 ， 稅 後EPS+27.55 元。 2Q26 稼
動 率 維 持 滿 載 ， 受 惠AI 伺 服 器 需 求 帶 動 ，CCL M7 以 上 產 品 高 階
品 項 營 收 比 重 提 升 至60-65% ， 預 計 2H26 CCL 漲 價 持 續 帶 動 營 運
獲利增長。 3Q26 與 2H26 展望，台光電 7 月 營 收(192.06 億元 )、8
月營收 (201.45 億元 )，3Q26 累 計 達 成 率 約69%，符 合 市 場 預 期。由
於 2026 年 6 月 發 布 之 漲 價 效 應 可 反 應 在3Q26 毛 利 率 ， 預 估 台 光
電 3Q26 營收為 573.32 億 元，+21.27%QoQ ，+127.99%YoY ，毛 利 率
35.08% ， 稅 後 淨 利132.02 億 元 ， 單 季 稅 後EPS+36.85 元。

二. AI 伺服器帶動 CCL/PCB 產值提升，產能擴充 60% 
宏遠投顧觀察 ，AI 伺 服 器 與 先 進 封 裝 等 需 求 日 增，高 階CCL 市場
需求將在 2024 年~2026 年 CAGR 達 26%， 而 高 階CCL 供 給 端 CAGR
則是 7% ， 高 階 市 場 需 求 殷 切 。 高 階 銅 箔 基 板(包含 AI 伺服器和高
階 網 路 交 換 器 ， 自2024 年始採用 M8 CCL 材料； 2026 年 M8 材料
佔總體 CCL 市場 40% ，M8 材 料 佔 比 顯 著 高 於2022 年、 2023 年的
22% 和 26% 。 台 系 銅 箔 基 板 供 應 鏈 當 中 ，M6~M8 等 高 階 材 料 的 供
應廠商為 (2383) 台 光 電。回顧 近年來 台 光 電 產 能 爬 坡 速 度，2025 年
月產能 400~550 萬張、 2026 年 月 產 能615 萬 張 、 預 計2027 年 月 產
能從 700~800 萬張至 4Q27 增至 930 萬 張 、 預 計2028 年 底 月 產 能
達到 1,110 萬 張 ， 連 續4 年 年 產 能 擴 充 近60%。2027 年隨著 NVIDIA  
Rubin 平 台 將 帶 動CCL M8+ 、M9 材 料 出 貨 攀 升 ，AI ASIC CCL 拉
貨動能 呈現 50~60% 高 度 成 長，台 光 電 高 階CCL 新 產 能 釋 出，產 品
組合持續優化 。2027 年 起 ， 除 一 般 型 伺 服 器Intel Diamond Rapids

三. 台光電低軌道衛星板材， 2027 年隨發射進程量增  
台光電於 低 軌 道 衛 星 延 伸 的 商 機，回顧 2025 年 低 軌 衛 星 發 射 量 增
加至 3~3.5 萬顆 (Space X 與 Amazon LEO 發 射 量 ， 年 增 率 達
+45%YoY) ，台 光 電、華 通 等 廠 商 投 入 資 源 開 發 低 軌 道 衛 星PCB 板
材，可 望 受 惠 低 軌 衛 星 鋪 設 建 置 量 增。宏 遠 投 顧 觀 察，低 軌 衛 星 量
體 近年來 成 長 快 速 ，2027 年以前， S 客 戶 進 入 火 箭V2mini 轉換至
V3 版 本 切 換 期(4Q26 開 始 重 啟 新 版 本)，Amazon LEO 發 射 時 程預
計 2027 年 以 前 發 射 量1,500~1,600 顆、 2029 年 發 射 量 達3,200 顆，
長 期 低 軌 衛 星 仍 呈 現 成 長 。天上衛星 PCB 板 技 術 層 次 高 台 光 電 、
華通的市佔率高 )， 天 上 衛 星 PCB 板與相對應之地面接收站量增
長，帶動 2025 ~2027 年 低 軌 衛 星 產 能 足 以 支 應增加 +40~ 50%YoY 拉
貨動能 。低 軌 大 型S 客戶 V3 版 本 新 型 火 箭 搭 載 低 軌 衛 星，所 採 用
之 銅 箔 基 板 升 級 至M8 等 級，材 料 損 耗 要 求 降 低，訊 號 傳 出 完 整 性
標 準 提 高 ， 對 於CCL 、PCB(HDI) 、 玻 纖 與 銅 箔 等 規 格 預 期 同 步 升
級 ， 推 升 材 料 成 本 與PCB 產值。

四.預估 2026/2027 年稅後 EPS 120.77/204.85 元 
2026 年受惠 AI 伺 服 器及 ASIC 需 求 持 續 強 勁 的 帶 動 下 ， 台 光 電 為
銅 箔 基 板 領 導 廠 商 ， 將 受 惠AI Server 對 於 高 階 銅 箔 基 板強 勁 的 需
求 ， 因 而，預估 2026 年 台 光 電 營 收 為1,999.92 億 元 ，+112.17%YoY ，
毛利率 34.21% ，稅 後 淨 利 432.76 億 元，稅 後 EPS+120.77 元。展 望
2027 年，在 AI 伺服器 需 求 持 續 強 勁 的 帶 動 下 ， 帶 動 產 能 持 續 增
長， 預估 2027 年 台 光 電 營 收 為3,530.14 億 元，+76.57%YoY ，毛 利
率為 35.49% ， 稅 後 淨 利734.03 億 元 ， 稅 後EPS+204.85 元。

四.投資建議維持 買進，目標價 6,145 元 
宏 遠 投 顧 認 為，台 光 電營 運 重 點 在 於，營 運 動 能 於2026 年 起 加 速
啟 動，一 般 型 伺 服 器CPU 廠商 Intel 、AMD 均 有 新 品 帶 動 新 平 台 升
級 與 相 關 供 應 的 零 組 件 商 機，以 及 攻 入AI 伺 服 器、高 階 網 路 交 換
器的 CCL 、 低 軌 衛 星CCL 規 格 轉 換 成 長 可 期。 基 於 上 述 ， 給 予台
光電 投 資 建 議 為 買 進 ， 目 標 價6,145 元(30.00x 20 27 EPS) 。

一. 公司簡介  
台光電 (2383) 為 銅 箔 基 板 廠 商，主 要 產 製雙 面 印 刷 電 路 板 與 多 層 印
刷 電 路 板 之 銅 箔 基 板、內 層 銅 箔 基 板、黏 合 片，產 品 應 用 別當 中，
基礎建設佔 75~80%(AI 伺 服 器 佔55~60%、 一 般 型 伺 服 器 佔15~20%、
Switch 佔 15~20% 、LEO 佔 5~10%) 。 行 動 裝 置 佔10~15% 、 車 用 工
業 與 其 他 合 計5~10% 。

程上 HDI 經 過 多 次 壓 合 後，PP 在 熱 壓 多 次後，每 一 層 熱 膨 脹 係 數
不同，導致通孔 Crack 或翹曲，製作時容易產生爆板，因此對於
PCB 板廠製作良率會是一大技術難關，而金像電、健鼎等廠商於
高階 HDI 相 對 具 備 生 產 優 勢 與 競 爭 力 ， 相 對 應 台 光 電 、 台 燿 等 高
階 CCL 供 貨 與 升 級 也 成 為 製 程 一 大 瓶 頸 ；2025 年月產能 400~550
萬張、 2026 年 月 產 能615 萬張、預計 2027 年 月 產 能 從700~800 萬
張至 4Q27 增至 930 萬 張 、 預 計2028 年 底 月 產 能 達 到1,110 萬 張 ，
連續 4 年 年 產 能 擴 充 近60% 。2027 年隨著 Nvidia Rubin 平 台 將 帶
動 CCL M8+、M9 材 料 出 貨 攀 升 ，AI ASIC CCL 拉 貨 動 能呈現 50~60%
高 度 成 長 ， 台 光 電 高 階CCL 新 產 能 釋 出 ， 產 品 組 合 持 續 優 化。

針對 AI GPU 與 ASIC 發 展 進 程 ，Google TPU 出 貨 量 從2026 年 400
萬顆上修至 2027 年(V8 增量 )增至 800 萬 顆，倍 數 增 長。AWS ASIC 
預計 2027 年 出 貨 量 達330~350 萬 顆，增 長+21%YoY 。NVIDIA 體系
GPU ，Rubin 系 列 層 數 提 升 至24~26L ， 材 料 端 從M8/M8+ 往 更 高 規
格 升 級 ， 更 高 層 數 與 多 層 式 架 構 ， 促 使PCB 板 訊 號 走 線 、 供 電 完
整性、高速訊號之阻抗匹配控制、訊號傳輸完整性，皆帶動 PCB
與上游材料呈現升級態勢 。 除一般型伺服器 Intel Diamond Rapids
平 台 、AMD Zen6 Venice 平 台 採 用 PCIe Gen6 與 Ultra Low Loss 
CCL(M7) ，PCB 板 層 數 增 至20~24L ；2027 年 ， 隨 著1.6T Switch 逐
步導入 M8/M9 CCL(HLC 層數 46~52L 、玻纖 Low Dk2/Q 布、銅箔
HVLP4/5) ，預期 CCL 廠 商 產 品 規 格 顯 著 升 級 。

### Page 3

Taiwan Electronic Components & Equipment
17 September 2026 Citi Research
3
Asia Vital Components (3017.TW)
Buy | TP NT$4,350.00 from NT$3,800.00 | Price NT$3,185.00 (17 Sep 26 13:30)
Sequential growth intact, ASIC carrying the upside — Our checks and mgmt.
comments indicate Vera Rubin platform has begun contributing in small volumes
from Sep, with a further ramp expected in 4Q26E. The ongoing ASIC project is now
shifting progressively from air cooling to liquid cooling, while another ASIC project

enters mass production in 4Q26E, together the main growth drivers as new
capacity comes online through the quarter. We now forecast 3Q26E/4Q26E
revenue growth at 18%/12% QoQ. GPM should also improve on a sequential basis,
supported by yield improvement and a rising contribution from server and liquid
cooling products.
ASIC contribution could exceed 50% of liquid cooling revenue in 2027 — With
cold plate demand continuing to run ahead of supply, we expect AVC to undertake

another round of capacity expansion in 2027 to meet rising ASIC demand,
consistent with mgmt. ’s view that ASIC contribution catches up with GPU in 2027.
We also note ongoing discussion around double-sided cold plate adoption, with
several platforms at the R&D stage, which we see as a source of upside
momentum. Rubin Ultra spec remains under development, and we do not expect
the potential TDP adjustments toward a lower level to affect content value, as cold

plate content is driven more by design than by chip TDP itself. On a higher liquid
cooling contribution, we forecast 2027E revenue to grow another 50% YoY, with
GPM to further increase to 34.9% (+2.2ppts YoY).
Implications — We raise our 2026E/27E/28E earnings forecasts by 3%/13%/16% to
reflect the updated outlook, driven mainly by higher cold plate capacity and
shipment assumptions alongside a more constructive view on ASIC liquid cooling
demand. Our revised target price of NT$4,350 (from NT$3,800) remains based on

25x 2027E EPS. Given AVC’s position in the thermal sector and its valuation
following the recent share price pullback, we see a favorable risk-reward profile and
reiterate our Buy rating.

### Page 5

Taiwan Electronic Components & Equipment
17 September 2026 Citi Research
5
Auras Technology (3324.TWO)
Buy | TP NT$1,800.00 from NT$1,550.00 | Price NT$1,385.00 (17 Sep 26 15:00)
Liquid cooling ramp drives sequential acceleration into 4Q26 — Following Aug-
26 revenue, which pulled back from a high base in Jul-26, we believe Auras remains
on track to meet mgmt. ’s earlier guidance of at least 15% QoQ growth in 3Q26E.
Into 4Q26, shipments of Vera Rubin products, including inner/rack manifolds,

together with the start of mass production for liquid cooling version of ASIC
project, should allow Auras to deliver another solid sequential growth. We now
forecast 3Q26E/4Q26E revenue growth of 17%/16% QoQ, with GPM also on an
upward trend, supported by a higher liquid cooling revenue mix.
GPU & ASIC projects broaden the 2027 growth base — Our checks and mgmt.
comments  indicate Auras is still working through cold plate qualification for the
Vera Rubin platform and is now participating in next generation product

development. Given its operating scale, we believe even a modest share gain could
contribute meaningfully to its top line, and we expect the earliest potential
contribution from 1Q27E. Auras should also benefit from rising ASIC liquid cooling
demand, with existing ASIC project shipments to expand above 4Q26E levels and
several further projects now under discussion. We forecast 2027E revenue growth
of 35% YoY, with liquid cooling mix rising from 61% in 2026E to 70% in 2027E and

GPM reaching 31.3% (+1.2ppts YoY).
Implications — We raise our 2026E/27E/28E earnings forecasts by 2%/14%/17% to
reflect the updated outlook, driven mainly by higher cold plate shipment
assumptions alongside a more constructive view on ASIC liquid cooling demand.
Our revised target price of NT$1,800 (from NT$1,550) remains based on 20x 2027E
EPS. Given Auras’ structural growth in the server business, widening liquid cooling
content across both GPU and ASIC platforms, and an optimizing global

manufacturing footprint in Thailand, we view the current valuation as attractive
and reiterate our Buy rating.

Asia Vital Components
Company description
Asia Vital Components (AVC), founded in 1991, is a global leader in thermal
solutions, originally focusing on PCs but now specializing in servers and
networking. With Furukawa Electric (Japan) as its largest shareholder, AVC
provides a comprehensive range of cooling components, including vapor
chambers (VCs), fans, cold plates, CDUs, and manifolds, as well as chassis

and assembly services for PCs and servers, enabling holistic thermal solution
designs and one-stop shop services. AVC's diverse product portfolio supports
various sectors, including consumer IT, cloud infrastructure, transportation,
energy and networking.
Investment strategy
We rate AVC a Buy. AVC is well-positioned for the liquid-cooling transition,
with leadership in cold plates and broad participation across Nvidia and CSP
projects. Its high-volume machining capability, manufacturing uniformity,

and Vietnam-based capacity provide it with a defensible position as
Blackwell and future deployments expand. The company captures a wider
share of the thermal BOM, across coldplates, manifolds, QDs, and CDU
opportunities, providing a multi-layered earnings engine.
Valuation
We value AVC using a PE-based approach that factors in earnings momentum
and the expansion of its addressable market. Our TP of NT$4,350 is derived
from applying a 25x PE multiple on 2027E EPS, which we believe is justified

given the company’s entrenched positioned in the server thermal segment,
rising contribution from liquid cooling, and the increasing value of thermal
content per server as power density continues to climb. With these structural
drivers still in place, we expect the multiple to hold at a premium relative to its
historical averages.
Risks
This stock is High Risk based upon our quantitative model but assigning a
High Risk rating is not supported by other qualitative factors such as cold-

plate leadership and deep CSP penetration, and so a High Risk rating has not
been applied. Key downside risks to our investment thesis and target price
include: 1) slower adoption pace for liquid cooling adoption; 2) heightened
price competition; 3) lower-than-expected share in liquid cooling
components; and 4) emerging alternative cooling technologies.
Auras Technology
Company description
Auras Technology was founded in 1998 and focused on the design and

manufacturing of thermal modules for NB/PCs. It quickly became one of
Taiwan’s top two NB cooling component suppliers. Early products included
heatsinks, fins, heat pipes, and fans for NBs, PCs, and smartphones. As
device form factor evolved, the company invested in new technologies such
Prepared for Kevin Lu

### Page 12

Taiwan Electronic Components & Equipment
17 September 2026 Citi Research
12
as ultra-thin vapor chamber in the 2000s to address the thermal challenges
of increasingly compact consumer electronics. During the 2010s, Auras
expanded into graphic card and server cooling and was an early adopter of
liquid cooling technology. By 2012, the company had begun developing liquid
cooling component and was among the first Taiwanese firms to venture into
AI server liquid cooling.

Investment strategy
We rate Auras a Buy. Auras is emerging as a key liquid-cooling supplier as
hyperscalers expand deployment. The company has secured qualifications for
cold plates and manifolds, opening access to a wider slate of Nvidia and CSP
projects. Thailand-based production offers non-China capacity at a scale
aligned with hyperscaler sourcing plans, allowing Auras to compete more
effectively for next-generation products.
Valuation
We value Auras using a PE-based approach, reflecting its accelerating

earnings trajectory and rising share in liquid cooling market of AI servers. Our
TP of NT$1,800 is based on 20x 2027E EPS, which we believe is justified
given the company’s expanding server revenue base, deeper engagement
with CSP customers, and broader product portfolio across cold plates, CDMs,
and CDUs.
Risks
This stock is High Risk based upon our quantitative model, but assigning a
High Risk rating is not supported in our view by other qualitative factors such

as rapid RVL progress and Thailand expansion, and so a High Risk rating has
not been applied. Key downside risks to our investment thesis and target
price include: 1) slower adoption of liquid cooling; 2) heightened price
competition; 3) lower-than-expected share in liquid cooling components;
and 4) emerging alternative cooling technologies.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

Wiwynn (6669.TW): New ASIC and GPU AI server racks in expansion; 
Aug strong beat; TP up to NT$3,777; Buy
21 September 2026 | 6:21AM HKT
Wiwynn delivered strong Aug revenues, which were 27% ahead of our estimates, 
reflecting strong general servers demand from US leading CSPs, and its major 
customers’ new ASIC AI servers ramping up. We expect sequential revenues growth 
to continue through Dec 2026, leading 3Q26 / 4Q26 revenues to grow at +46% / 
+22% QoQ, driven by (1) major customers’ new ASIC AI servers ramping up, across 
air-cooled and liquid-cooled, (2) general servers end demand remaining solid on 
agentic AI, (3) new GPU-powered AI server racks entering mass production in 4Q26 
across NVIDIA and AMD platforms, and (4) potential new customers in ASIC AI 
servers. Maintain Buy with TP raised to NT$3,777 (21.5x 2027E P/E vs. +32% EPS YoY 
in 2027-28E).

Ongoing capacity expansion: As we highlighted in Aug (report link), Wiwynn 
announced a 2H26 capex budget of US$942m, implying 2026 capex could be up to 
NT$40bn (US$1.3bn). On Sep 16, the company announced equipment procurement 
of US$16m (NT$512m), reflecting the strong AI demand and the company’s 
commitments on capacity expansion. We estimate the company’s capex at NT$44bn 
in 2026E, and to stay at a similar high level through 2028E, supporting its production 
sites diversification across the US, Mexico, and Malaysia, AI servers expansion from 
ASIC to GPU-powered, and products expansion across different ASICs and GPUs, 
along with further customers penetration. 
Earnings revision: We factor in August revenues, and change our net income by +7% 
/ +5% / +3% in 2026-28E. The change is mainly on revenues: we raise 2026-28E 
revenues by 8% / 7% / 5%, reflecting the strong beat of Aug revenues driven by

growing AI demand, which is also driving its general servers growth. Our EPS are reduced 
as we update the share count, reflecting the company’s stock split that started from 
Sept 2026. The mathematical effect on our EPS is around 66%.

Valuation: We continue to derive our 12M TP based on near-term P/E, and our 2027E 
target P/E increases slightly to 21.5x (vs. 20x previously), which is still derived from the 
peers’ updated correlation of forward trading P/E and EPS growth. Accordingly, our 12M 
TP increases by 13% to NT$3,777.00 (vs. NT$3,352.55 previously). Our target P/E of 
21.5x is above the company’s historical avg.+1stv. forward trading P/E, reflecting our 
positive view on Wiwynn’s leading position in ASIC AI server ODM along with product 
expansion (from ASIC to GPU AI servers and general server) and customers expansion. 
Maintain Buy.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Wiwynn 
We are Buy-rated on Wiwynn with a 12-month TP of NT$3,777. Our target price is 
based on 21.5x 2027E P/E, which is derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E 
multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s performance. 
Key downside risks: 1) weaker-than-expected AI server demand growth; 2) 
slower-than-expected recovery in general server demand; and 3) more severe 
competition in the server ODM market.

## Document 079: 260922_ubs_memory smartphone

Source PDF: all report pdf/260922_ubs_memory smartphone.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-22
Pages: 14
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

ab 22 September 2026
Global Research
Global I/O
Smartphones
Memory to continue to weigh in on
Smartphones in 2027
Smartphone unit Sell-In -3% YoY in '27E; initial '28E forecasts at +2% YoY
UBS 3Q26 APAC Tech Tour takeaways ( link) continue to point towards (1) further
downside for Android ex-Samsung smartphone unit builds into 2027, and (2) relative
resilience for Apple and Samsung. Memory procurement could turn out to be
incrementally more challenging for smartphone OEMs in 2027 than in 2026. Memory

makers continue to prioritise hyperscale and AI customers. Hence memory supply could
be even more of a bottleneck next year. As such, we further de-risk our '27E
smartphone unit Sell-In forecasts to 1.11bn (-3% YoY) from 1.16bn (+2% YoY)
prior and roll out initial '28E forecasts at 1.13bn (+2% YoY). We leave our '26E
forecasts unchanged at 1.14bn (-10% YoY).
Memory costs continue to go higher
We now estimate memory to account for 35% of the bill of material (BOM) costs of

flagship/premium smartphones and 56% of those of low-end smartphones by 4Q26E
(from 13%/29% in 4Q25), based on our current base case scenario of the DRAM/NAND
upcycle extending until at least 2Q28E/4Q27E. This is broadly consistent with Xiaomi's
commentary that memory now accounts for 40% of average smartphone BOM costs
and 50-60% of low-end BOM costs. We believe the current memory cycle will remain a
key challenge for OEMs as (1) increasing costs continue to pressure margins, and (2) ASP

hikes aiming to offset such pressures are likely to pose incremental downside risk to end
demand. Over the long term, we believe cost control will be more manageable for Apple
and, to a lesser extent, Samsung, given their premium product portfolio, while more
detrimental for others.
Apple & Samsung outperformance likely to continue
Global smartphone unit Sell-Through (source: Counterpoint) is now down 8.4% YoY Jul
'26 YTD. Within this, we note Apple (+8.9% YoY) and Samsung (-4.8% YoY; Sell-In up

YoY implies some channel inventory build) continue to outperform their Chinese
Android peers (Vivo/Xiaomi/Oppo/Transsion: -16.4%/-17.9%/-18.4%/-22.4% YoY).
With premium models like to fare better in this downturn, we believe Apple and, to a
lesser extent, Samsung will continue to outperform the broader market and gain
incremental share. iPhone suppliers continue to expect '27E to be a strong year for
Apple, with the iPhone Duo (c.10m units in first 12M) as well as the new launch

schedule likely to drive YoY unit growth. Samsung continues to believe it will be able to
grow unit and gain market share, though profitability will remain a concern.
Selective on smartphone plays; prefer memory/MLCC suppliers
Within the smartphone supply chain, we continue to prefer MediaTek and Samsung
(both APAC Key Call Buy) as well as ASE, Hon Hai, JCET, Micron, Murata, SEMCO,
SK Hynix and TSMC (all Buy). We remain cautious on LG Display, Maxscend, Xiaomi (all

Valuation Method and Risk Statement
The handset industry, as part of consumer electronics, is cyclically sensitive to the economy
and so upside and downside risks relate to macro drivers. Other downside risks include the
rapid loss of market share due to poor product timing, design or distribution. The supply
chain companies into the handset industry also operate in a highly cyclical market both from
macro concerns and inventory cycles within the supply chain. These companies also operate

in a highly competitive environment with product innovation and R&D investments critical to
success. Valuation methods across the sector include multiple analysis.

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is defined as expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over
the next 12 months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA) is defined as
the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.
EXCEPTIONS AND SPECIAL CASES:UK and European Investment Fund ratings and definitions are: Buy: Positive on factors
such as structure, management, performance record, discount; Neutral: Neutral on factors such as structure, management,
performance record, discount; Sell: Negative on factors such as structure, management, performance record, discount. Core

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

### Page 3

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Gigabyte 
We are Neutral rated on Gigabyte with a 12-month target price of NT$487. Our TP is 
based on a 14.1x 2027E P/E which is based on PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E 
multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s share 
performance. 
Key upside/downside risks include: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market 
recovery, 2) faster-/slower-than-expected gaming PC growth, and 3) 
faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up.

Source: Company data, Goldman Sachs Research estimates, FactSet. Price as of 24 Sep 2026 close.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

Compal (2324.TW): AI servers to fuel 4Q26E/1Q27E growth; Aug miss; 
Neutral
27 September 2026 | 6:06PM HKT
3-month revenues preview: We expect sequential revenue growth in Sep-Dec, 
considering the company’s rising AI server shipments, AI server capacity expansion, 
and PC product mix upgrade towards high end model (e.g. AI PC). Overall, we expect 
Compal’s 3Q / 4Q26E revenues to grow at 1% / 16% QoQ, riding on AI server racks 
ramp up and the company’s expansion from motherboard (L6) to system (L10 / L11). 
We remain positive on Compal’s business diversification, supported by global 
capacity expansion in the US, Taiwan, Vietnam and China, while consumer 
electronics still accounts for the majority of the company’s total revenue, for which 
demand could be capped by rising raw material costs (e.g., memory, CPU). Maintain 
Neutral on fair valuation.

Compal’s Aug revenue was up 15% YoY / down 17% MoM to NT$67bn, or 20% lower 
than our estimate, reflecting PC shipments decline. Aug PC shipments was down 22% 
MoM to 1.4m units, reflecting the impact of 1H26 demand pull-in amid the rising 
memory cost and weak consumer electronics demand.

Earnings revision: Factoring in Compal’s Aug revenue, we cut our 2026-28E net

### Page 3

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Compal 
We are Neutral-rated on Compal with a 12-month target price of NT$46.1. Our target 
price is based on 14.7x 2027E P/E, which is derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth 
vs. P/E multiple correlation. We see EPS growth and market re-rating for ODM 
companies as major factors for the stock’s performance.  
Key upside/downside risks: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market recovery; 
2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and 3) 
stronger/weaker-than-expected demand for tablets.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

Vanguard International Semi.
Valuation
Our target price for VIS of NT$185 is based on 25x 2027E P/E, which we see as justified by its robust PMIC sales growth
momentum and increasing outflow order opportunity from TSMC with support from its consistent cash dividend policy. Our
target price equates to 2026/27E P/B of 3.6x/3.5x.
Risks
Key downside risks to our target price include: 1) weaker 8-inch demand along with higher costs due to inflation or more severe

ASP competition from Chinese peers; 2) lower UTR of the new capacity at the newly acquired fab, with depreciation burden;
and 3) less-than-expected outsourcing opportunities from IDMs.
NXP Semiconductors NV
(NXPI.O; US$238.08; 1; 25 Sep 26; 16:00)
Valuation
Our price target of $370 is based on a 17x P/E applied to CY28 EPS, given peer multiple expansion tied to rising data center
exposure.
Risks
End Market: NXP derives roughly 55% of sales from the automotive end market and roughly 18% of sales from the industrial

end market. Therefore, any major changes in auto sales or industrial activity could result in upside/downside to our estimates
and target price for NXP.
Competition: NXP competes with several companies in analog, digital, and microcontrollers. Consequently, any changes in
market share could result in upside/downside risk to our estimates and target price.
Inventory Risk: NXP sells roughly 50-60% of its products through distributors. As a result, we believe any major fluctuations of

inventory in the distribution channel could result in upside/downside risk to our estimates and target price.
Macroeconomic: NXP’s geographic exposure spans multiple geographies including the U.S., Europe, and Asia. As a result, any
prolonged macroeconomic strength/weakness could result in upside/downside to our estimates and target price.
TSMC
(2330.TW; NT$2475.0; 1; 24 Sep 26; 13:30)
Valuation
Our target price for TSMC shares of NT$3,800 is based on 25x the average of our 2027E EPS, higher than its three-year

average forward PER, versus global peers’ average of 15-23x. We believe our PER target is justified by TSMC's leading position
in advanced process node and robust AI demand growth outlook. Given its continuing leadership in the semiconductor
foundry industry, we believe TSMC’s stable order visibility and earnings outlook make it less vulnerable than peers to a
potential global economic downturn. Our target price equates to 2026E/27E P/E of 34x/25x and 2026E/27E P/B of 13x/10x.

Risks
Key downside risks that could impede the shares from achieving our target price include: 1) weakness in the global
semiconductor market; 2) larger-than-expected margin contractions due to depreciation cost hikes and strong NTD; 3)
competitors entering the foundry business; 4) longer-than-expected digestion of inventory in the supply chain; and 5) a
slowdown in demand either from a global economic downturn or a global trade disruption triggered by tariffs.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.
Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk

rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment
rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered

stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating
and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also

suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price
chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in

exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the
expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility

or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:
Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside

call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is
moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day

period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be
different from and does not affect a stock’s fundamental equity rating, which reflects a longer-term total absolute return

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

### Page 1

ab 28 September 2026
Global Research
Nan Ya Plastics
Buy on CCL price hikes and AI-led mix upgrade
Assume coverage with a  Buy and a higher price target of NT$300.00
We assume coverage of Nan Ya Plastics (NYP)  with a Buy and PT of NT$300.  The share
price is up c310% YTD, while Nan Ya PCB (NYPCB),  61% owned by NYP, is up c420%
YTD.  We believe most of NYP's share price appreciation has been driven by the value of
its listed holdings rather than its core Copper Clad Laminate (CCL) business. We

continue to flag earnings upside potential from CCL, driven by ASP increases of
90%/85% in 2026/27E and operating margin expansion from 4.1% in 2025 to
23%/30% in 2026/27E (H126 at c17%). Key drivers include supply led price hikes, a
richer AI product mix and NYP's integrated CCL value chain. In petrochemicals, we
expect the MEG-ethylene spread to turn positive in Q326, with 2026-27E spreads aided
by geopolitical supply tightness. Our 2026/27E earnings are 15%/23% above

Bloomberg consensus. Further upstream tightness and high-end AI CCL order wins are
the upside catalysts.
CCL ASP up 90%/85% in 2026/27E
We expect CCL ASP to rise 90%/85% YoY in 2026/27E:  c80%/55% from supply led
price hikes (see UBS  Tech team's CCL sector report) and c10%/30% from product mix
upgrades. Conventional CCL prices should be supported by AI capacity crowding out
commodity grades, higher material intensity and limited near-term upstream additions.

Industry CCL prices are up 100% in 2026 YTD;  part should flow into 2027E as contracts
reset. We expect M6+ revenue contribution to rise from 8% of CCL revenue in Q326 to
25% in 2027E with ASPs 3-10x higher than conventional FR4 products.
Vertical integration and ASP increase to support 23-30% CCL OPM
NYP's CCL chain covers  glass cloth, epoxy resin and copper foil. We view epoxy resin and
copper foil as fully self-supplied and most glass cloth as internally sourced. This should

support value capture as CCL prices rise. Based on historical sensitivity, we estimate that
a US$1.00 increase in CCL ASP would translate to an incremental US$0.45/0.37 per
piece increase in operating profit in 2026/27E. As a result, we forecast CCL OPM of
23%/30% in 2026/27. In-house material R&D could accelerate AI CCL qualification,
while the partnership with Nittobo would secure Gen2 low-Dk glass cloth and support
the ramp of high-end products.

Valuation:  new price target of NT$300.00
We value NYP using SOTP:  the UBS PT of NT$1,150 for NYPCB; 19x 2027E EV/EBITDA
for non-PCB electronic materials (CCL and upstream); 10x 2027E EV/EBITDA for
petchem (chemicals, plastics and polyester), all in line with peers. Listed stakes (FPCC,
FPC, FCFC, NYT, Mai Liao Power and others)  at UBS's PTs, market values, or peer
multiples. After a 10% HoldCo discount, our PT is NT$300.00, implying 15x 2027E PE,

0.3x PEG and 4.4x P/BV.

### Page 2

Nan Ya Plastics 28 September 2026 ab 2
Nan Ya Plastics UBS Research
Thesis Map     UBS                    Research     THESIS MAP a guide to our thinking and what´s where in this report
Q: Will  CCL and electronic materials (EM) become the  key profit drivers?
Yes. We expect EM revenue to rise from 46% in 2025 to 59%/69% in 2026/27E, and to account for
nearly all EBIT. Within EM, CCL would be the growth engine, up from 24% of EM revenue  in 2025 to

31%/43% in 2026/27E.
Q: Will the conventional CCL upcycle last through 2027?
Yes. UBS's Tech team expects conventional CCL prices and profits to keep rising in 2027E on: 1) high-
end AI grades taking  capacity from commodity CCL;  2) higher material intensity  in AI-CCL lifting
upstream demand; and 3) tight weaving capacity limiting near-term capacity additions.
Q: Can NYP achieve higher volume in AI-CCL
Yes. Vertical integration would likely help "recipe" changes and customer qualification. The Nittobo

tie-up should secure low-Dk glass and support high-end ramp.
We assume coverage of NYP with a Buy rating and a PT of NT$300.00. The share price is up c310%
YTD, while NYPCB, 61%-owned by NYP, is up c420% YTD. We think most of the share price
movement reflects its listed holdings rather than a full re-rating of the core CCL business. We
continue to expect earnings upside from CCL ASP growth of 90%/85% and CCL OPM expansion
from 4.1% in 2025 to 23%/30% in 2026/27E (H126 at c17%) , driven by supply led price hikes, a

richer AI mix and its integrated CCL value chain. For petchem, we expect the MEG-ethylene spread to
turn positive in Q326E, with 2026/27E margins supported by geopolitical led supply tightness.
Further upstream tightness and high-end AI CCL order wins are the key upside catalysts.
1) Conventional CCL prices are up nearly 100% YTD vs. 2025;  hikes continue to occur on a monthly
basis. 2) CCL and glass cloth are running at close to full capacity (c90% utilisation). An increase in

capacity  of 6.2m pcs/month in Q326 to 6.5m by Q426; 6.7-7.0m by end-2027 according to the
company. 3) M7 and M8 low-/ultra low-loss CCL products are now listed on the company's website
(updated in September 2026). 4) The November 2025 partnership with Nittobo should help the
company secure next-gen low-Dk glass yarn supply  for high-end AI CCL.
NYP is trading at 12x 2027E PE and 3.6x 2027E P/BV, significantly below EM peers at 36x 2027E PE
and 8.2x P/BV.  Our PT of NT$300.00 implies 15x 2027E PE and 4.4x 2027E P/BV. We believe the gap

reflects concerns about petchem, rather than improved CCL profitability.

Nan Ya Plastics one of the core companies of Taiwan's Formosa Group. Its businesses span electronic
materials, chemicals, plastics processing and polyester. NYP is one of the largest CCL producers, with

a vertically integrated CCL value chain, supplying CCL to its 61%-owned subsidiary Nan Ya PCB and

NYP CCL: a vertically integrated traditional CCL leader; AI
CCL ramping
Copper foil, epoxy resin and glass fibre are the three major materials for CCL, accounting
for 42%, 26% and 19% of conventional CCL costs, respectively, according to
Trendforce. As CCL migrates to higher end AI grades, we expect glass fibre and specialty
resin to take a larger share of total BOM cost, mainly driven by higher value low-Dk glass

and low-loss resin.
NYP has a highly vertically integrated CCL supply chain with in-house production
spanning glass yarn, glass fibre, epoxy resin, copper foil, and CCL. We believe vertical
integration differentiates NYP from other CCL companies given: 1) Provides supply
security: during periods of tight raw material supply, NYP can rely on internal key
material capacity, thereby reducing procurement risk and supporting stable production.
2) Faster customer qualification : with key upstream materials produced in-house,

NYP can adjust formulations and specifications more quickly to meet customer-specific
performance requirements during high-end CCL qualification. 3) Better cost control:
internal sourcing allows NYP to capture more value across the supply chain.

AI bandwidth growth boosts high-end CCL demand
As AI chips become more powerful ( Blackwell to NVIDIA's Rubin and Vera Rubin ), the
chips need to send more data at much faster speeds. The faster the data travels, the
easier it is for the signal to weaken or lose quality.  This means AI servers need better

circuit board materials to keep data moving quickly and reliably. High-end CCL, such as
M8 and M9, reduces this signal loss and therefore is becoming increasingly important as
AI computing power and data speeds continue to rise.

Vertical integration supports higher OPM
We compared CCL profitability with KBL (Kingboard Laminates, 1888.HK; covered by
UBS analyst Edward Liu), NYP and ITEQ (ITEQ Corporation, 6213.TW; Not Covered) and

note that vertical integration appears to support a higher margin. Fully integrated across
the three key CCL upstream materials, KBL delivered a 26% OPM in H126, followed by
17% for NYP,   and 11% for ITEQ which primarily sources upstream materials externally.
While product mix differs across peers, we believe companies with vertical integrated
supply chains are able to deliver better cost controls for raw materials and thus capture
higher value across the supply chain, particularly as upstream prices rise.

Supply-driven price upcycle to continue
Despite lukewarm consumer electronics demand, conventional CCL remains in a strong
pricing upcycle. The company has said that prices for mid and low-end CCL continue to
rise 5-10% per month, while certain grades are recording monthly increases of 10-15%.

The current supply shortfall reflects a combination of the following:
1) Capacity migration to high-end AI materials. As industry companies increasingly
prioritise AI-related products, a growing portion of CCL capacity is being reallocated
from conventional grades. High-end materials also require significantly longer
processing times and lower throughput, effectively reducing industry-wide supply of
traditional CCL.
2) Rising material intensity and order spillover. Increasing PCB complexity, including

higher layer counts in AI servers and networking equipment, is structurally raising CCL
consumption per unit. Concurrently, leading high-end CCL suppliers are focusing on
premium products, resulting in order spillover into the conventional CCL market and
tightening supply further.
3) Persistent upstream bottlenecks.  Tight key raw material supply, particularly for
glass fibre cloth and copper foil, continues to constrain effective capacity additions. The

limited availability of electronic grade glass cloth, long lead times for weaving
equipment and emerging shortages in high-end copper foil have extended the supply
crunch across the broader CCL value chain.
As the AI cycle continues, UBS's Tech team expects industry supply-demand conditions
to remain tight, at least through 2027E as capacity expansion requires multiple years to
materialise and is unlikely to keep pace with demand growth, in our view. As a result, we

expect conventional CCL pricing to remain on an upward trajectory, supporting further
margin expansion across the traditional CCL value chain.

Supply-driven price hikes to drive 80%/55% ASP growth in 2026/27E
Conventional CCL pricing has entered a sharp supply-driven upcycle. According to

TPCA,  Kingboard's FR4 price hikes exceeded 50% cumulatively by July, while local media
estimated YTD increases had exceeded 100% by end-August following further hikes.
NYP also confirmed 100% YTD price hikes for a number of conventional CCL products.
According to the Panasonic Industry Official Price Announcement , Panasonic
announced  three rounds of price increases for general glass-epoxy CCL in 2026 of 30%,
20% and 30%, equivalent to 103% on a compounded basis.

NYP management noted that the 26Q4 pricing upcycle remains intact, with CCL prices
rising 5-10% monthly, while some grades have increased more than 100% cumulatively
since 2025. KBL also confirmed that conventional CCL prices have more than doubled
versus end-2025. KBL raised prices twice in April and accelerated monthly increases to
15% in June and July.
Historically, NYP's CCL ASP has moved in the same direction as KBL. Based on the UBS
Tech team's sector view, we forecast CCL ASP price increases of 80%/55% in 2026/27,

driven purely by pricing. We flag the announced industry price hikes of up to 100% are
not immediately fully reflected in realised ASP, as implementation varies by customer and
often requires contract renewal or price renegotiation.

EMC anticipates undersupply to continue in the next two years
At the UBS Taiwan Summit, EMC (2383.TW, Not Covered) anticipated further price hikes
in H226 based on rising component costs, but that the magnitude of the increase would
likely be less than in H126. Management noted equipment lead time currently is one to
two years and that high-end CCL demand has not slowed. It thus anticipates

undersupply to continue in the next two years.
High-end CCL: next-gen AI rack architecture to lift CCL content and ASP
High-end AI CCL is mainly used in AI server compute boards, switch boards and
midplane/backplanes, where higher data transmission speeds require low-loss laminate
materials. Using NVIDIA as an example as its rack architecture is increasingly PCB/CCL
intensive, the current VR200 system is built on an Oberon NVL72 rack design, while

### Page 9

Nan Ya Plastics 28 September 2026 ab 9
future generations could adopt the Kyber rack, which would increase the NVLink
domain per rack to 144 or above GPU.
Compared with current Oberon architecture, Kyber should increase CCL content per
rack as it supports twice or above GPUs and relies on increasingly complex, high layer
count PCB interconnects. This should drive both higher CCL volume and a richer mix of
advanced low-loss materials, supporting higher CCL value per rack. High-end CCL share

matters disproportionately for revenue because M8+ ASP can be several times higher
than conventional FR4.
While our base case assumes continued adoption of higher-end AI CCL, supply-chain
feedback suggests Nvidia's Kyber rack architecture may be delayed beyond Rubin Ultra,
potentially extending the use of the current Oberon platform through much of 2028.
This could defer part of the expected AI-related materials upgrade cycle. However, we
see a potential offset from ASIC programs, with Google TPU v9 and Amazon Trainium 4

expected to transition from 2-die to 4-die architectures from late 2027. The resulting
increase in bandwidth requirements and system complexity could support incremental
demand for PCB/CCL materials, partially mitigating the impact of a slower Kyber
transition.
AI CCL product mix upgrade to drive 10%/30% ASP expansion in
2026/27E
We expect M6+ CCL volume to increase from 0% in 2025 to 2% in 2026E and 5% in
2027E. We think this would be supported by: 1) NYP starting M7/8 CCL shipments,

while M9/10 are undergoing customer qualification, according to a news report; 2) the
company is ramping up capacity to meet increasing demand (Figure 5); and 3) improving
low-Dk glass supply through its partnership with Nittobo.
We model M6&M6+ ASP at 3-10x higher than conventional FR4; we forecast the
expansion in high-end CCL volume would result in a 10% and 30% YoY increases in
blended ASP.

Partnership with Nittobo to strengthen NYP's position in high-end AI
CCL
Nittobo is a leading supplier of specialty electronic glass fibre cloth for advanced AI and

high-speed applications. According to TrendForce, it holds approximately 90% of the
global T-glass market and 60-70% of the next-generation low-Dk glass market, while
Nittobo describes its NE-glass (low dielectric specialty glass) as the de facto standard for
low-Dk applications.
In November 2025, Nittobo and NYP announced a strategic partnership under which
Nittobo will provide NYP with a long term and stable supply of next-gen low-Dk glass
yarn (the upstream material for glass cloth), while NYP will provide specialty glass cloth

weaving capacity by utilising the company's 4,000 weaving machine capacity.
We expect the partnership to accelerate NYP's move into high-end AI CCL, as it secures
access to a scare upstream material, reduces supply bottleneck risk, and should improve
the company's ability to develop and qualify higher end low-loss CCL products.

We expect CCL OPM to rise to 23%/30% in 2026/27E
Historically, a US$1.00 per piece increase in CCL ASP results in a US$0.23/0.43 rise in
CCL operating profit per piece. Based on this, we estimate that a US$1.00 ASP increase
would translate to a  US$0.45 per piece incremental OP in 2026E, as price hikes and
operating leverage allow a larger portion of ASP gains to translate to profit. We expect

### Page 11

Nan Ya Plastics 28 September 2026 ab 11
flow through to moderate in 2027E at $0.37, as ASP growth is increasingly driven by
higher end CCL products, which have a lower scale compared to conventional products
and higher raw material costs.

Comparison with consensus. Our 2026E/27 net profit forecasts are 15%/23% above
consensus. This is primarily driven by our EBIT estimates, which are 21%/30% above
consensus as we expect 90%/85% increases in CCL ASP in 2026/27E to significantly
expand OP margins. As a result, we expect the company's CCL OPM to rise from c4% in

2025 to 23%/30% in 2026/27E, underpinned by higher ASP and the company’s cost
control capabilities within its vertically integrated supply chain.
In addition, our 2026E/27 investment income forecasts are 10%/23% higher than
consensus, largely reflecting higher equity income contributions from Nanya Technology
(NYT, 2408.TW, Buy, covered by UBS analyst Nicolas Gaudois), in which the company
holds a 29% stake.
Comparison with historical valuation. The stock is currently trading at 14x P/E based

on 12-month forward consensus estimates, 1SD below its five-year historical average
PE. It trades at  3.9x P/BV on 12-month forward consensus estimates, supported by a
21% 12-month forward ROE. Both P/BV and ROE are at five-year historical highs.
NYP owns a 61% equity stake in NYPCB (8046.TW), a 29% stake in NYT (2408.TW),
23% of FPCC (Formosa Petrochemical Corporation, 6505.TW), 4.6% of FPC (Formosa
Plastics Corporation, 1301.TW) and 2.4% of FCFC (Formosa Chemicals & Fibre

Corporation, 1326.TW). Excluding the market value of these listed companies and
assuming a stable holdco discount over time, we view the residual value as representing
NYP’s core operations  (CCL and the upstream business, as well as the company’s
petchem business). NYP's implied core business currently accounts for 33% of its market
cap, approximately 1SD above its historical average. However, we estimate CCL and
petrochemicals will contribute c30% of earnings in 2027E and c50% by 2030E. We

believe the market is undervaluing NYP’s core business, as concerns on its petchem
segment are overshadow the company’s transition toward faster-growing, higher-value
CCL earnings.
Comparison with sector peers. NYP trades at 12x 2027E PE and 3.6x 2027E P/BV on
our estimates, compared to 36x 2027E PE and 8.2x P/BV for its EM peers. We believe the
market undervalues the company’s expanding CCL business due to excessive concerns
about the legacy petchem business that could weigh on margins. We expect continued

geopolitical volatility to support MEG-ethylene spreads and to preserve petchem
profitability from 2026 to H127E.

Valuation: CCL business to drive valuation upside
We value Nan Ya  Plastics using a SOTP  valuation methodology.
Core business. For the company's PCB business, we use the UBS price target of NT
$1,150  (covered by UBS analyst Diana Chang). For non-PCB  EM, we value the business
at 19x 2027E EV/EBITDA, a discount to  industry  CCL upstream peers, reflecting lower
scarcity and pricing power than upstream bottleneck materials, but higher than CCL
companies, supported by more vertical integration and value capture than pure CCL

producers as well as a commitment to shareholder returns via dividend payments (not
less than a 50% payout ratio).  For the company's core chemicals, plastics and polyester
businesses, we assign 10x 2027E EV/EBITDA, in line with Asia petchem  peers.
Investments. For the company's investments in FPCC, FPC, FCFC, NYT, Mai Liao Power
and others,  we assume UBS price targets and market values where relevant for the listed
holdings, or we assume peer valuation multiples.

HoldCo discount. We apply a relatively modest 10% HoldCo discount to NYP, given
their transparent market values, visible dividend policies and consistent cash
distributions. NYP has demonstrated an ability and willingness to monetise the holdings.
In 2026, NYP sold a 19.4m equity stake in NYPCB for NT$13.4bn and also approved the
disposal of 31m NYT shares, demonstrating that these stakes are monetisable. Regular
dividends from the listed companies also allow NYP to realise part of their underlying

value without requiring outright disposal. Our sensitivity applies a 5-30% HoldCo
discount with 10% as our base case.
Our SOTP-derived PT of NT$300.00 implies 15x 2027E PE, 0.3x 27E PEG and a 4.4x P/BV.
We assume coverage of NYP with a Buy rating.

### Page 17

Nan Ya Plastics 28 September 2026 ab 17
Nan Ya Plastics UBS Research
Company Description
Company Description
Established in 1958 and listed on the TAIEX in 1967, Nan Ya
Plastics one of the core companies of Taiwan's Formosa Group.
Its businesses span electronic materials, chemicals, plastics
processing and polyester. NYP is one of the largest copper clad
laminates (CCL) producers, with a vertically integrated CCL value
chain, supplying CCL to its 61%-owned subsidiary Nan Ya PCB

and other printed circuit board (PCB) makers. It owns 29% of
Nan Ya Tech, the only Taiwan-based dynamic random access
memory (DRAM) brand. It also owns 23% of Formosa
Petrochemical.
Industry Outlook
The traditional FR4-alike CCL industry is in a supply-driven
upcycle with further pricing upside as AI continues to crowd out
conventional capacity, while high-end CCL is entering a multi-
year AI-driven growth cycle, led by specification upgrades and

increasing penetration of servers and networking. 2026
feedstock disruptions amid geopolitical tensions have caused
some temporary tightening, but the medium-term outlook
remains weak unless capacity rationalisation accelerates or end-
demand improves.

Company Description
Established in 1958 and listed on the TAIEX in 1967, Nan Ya Plastics one of the core
companies of Taiwan's Formosa Group. Its businesses span electronic materials, chemicals,
plastics processing and polyester. NYP is one of the largest copper clad laminates (CCL)

producers, with a vertically integrated CCL value chain, supplying CCL to its 61%-owned
subsidiary Nan Ya PCB and other printed circuit board (PCB) makers. It owns 29% of Nan Ya
Tech, the only Taiwan-based dynamic random access memory (DRAM) brand. It also owns
23% of Formosa Petrochemical.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Nan Ya Plastics:
We base our price target for NYP on a SOTP valuation.
Key upside risks include: 1) stronger CCL pricing; 2) faster high-end CCL mix upgrades; and 3)

a faster petchem margin recovery.
Key downside risks are: 1) a CCL pricing reversal on faster supply additions or weaker
demand; 2) slower AI qualification/ramp of the company's CCL business; and 3) further
weakening chemicals/polyester businesses.
Nan Ya Printed Circuit Board:
Our price target for Nan Ya Printed Circuit Board is based on P/E multiple methodology.
Upside risks include faster-than-expected ramp-up of networking/switches, stronger-than-

expected pricing increase, better-than-estimated consumer demand, and continued tightness
in BT substrates.
Downside risks include lower-than-expected ramp-up of networking/switch projects, lower-
than-expected global PC/server demand, oversupply from capacity expansions, raw material
cost increases, intensified competition, and a lack of spec upgrades and content increase.
As for industry risks, substrate applications are dominated by PCs, but servers, artificial

intelligence and advanced computing are driving demand growth. We believe key risks to the
industry include slower-than-expected growth in servers, networking, and high performance
computing, ASP pressure, margin erosion, and lack of spec upgrades. We believe the flexible
circuit board and high-density interconnect board segments are more vulnerable to
fluctuations in the consumer product cycle and have lower visibility.
Nanya Technology:
Nanya Tech is a pure-play DRAM manufacturer based in Taiwan mainly focused on legacy DDR

products for customers across various end applications. We think downside risks to our thesis
could include a potential slowdown in end demand and hence the conventional memory
cycle as well as Nanya's legacy-centric product portfolio. We value Nanya Tech shares using a
target NTM P/BV multiple based on long-term average ROE and cost of equity.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

## Document 101: 260929_ms_TDK-Taiyo-Yuden

Source PDF: all report pdf/260929_ms_TDK-Taiyo-Yuden.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-29
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

M
Update
Yageo Corp.  | Asia Pacific
TDK-Taiyo Yuden MOU: high-
end wall rises; cycle thesis
intact
The MOU validates AI-driven high-cap MLCC tightness, which
supports pricing and our MLCC up-cycle view.
TDK and Taiyo Yuden (both covered by Shoji Sato) signed an MOU to explore an
alliance in advanced MLCCs/inductors –  including manufacturing/procurement
cooperation, potential METI subsidies under Japan's Economic Security Act, and a
possible future capital tie-up.

Our take: We estimate MLCC demand next year at more than 40bn units of 47uF+,
which crowds out  midrange/low-end MLCC capacity.
1. We believe  de-specing to ≤22uF MLCCs is not a viable workaround.
Substituting high-cap MLCCs with larger counts of lower-capacitance parts
requires broad real estate that simply does not exist on today's densely
packed GPU/accelerator boards.
2. Switching to tantalum-polymer capacitors merely trades one shortage for
another, given equally tight tantalum supply.

3. So the only realistic industry response, in our view, is reallocating midrange/
low-end MLCC capacity toward high-cap production. That would structurally
tightens midrange/low-end supply, underpinning broad-based pricing power.
Things to watch:
• Whether a capital alliance materializes,
• METI subsidy size (this determines how distorted the high-end cost curve
gets),
• Counter-move by Murata (also covered by Shoji Sato) counter-move, and
• Any sign the alliance extends into mid-cap products.

See our latest report on MLCC here: Global Technology: MLCC: Continued Demand
Growth for High-Value-added Products for AI & Data Centers (15 Sep 2026)

STOCK RATINGS

Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

短期運力供給受限，惟傳統淡季將近 
 3Q26 美線運價優於預期 ， 預估稅後EPS 4.48 元：Aug-26 營收 216.11
億元(+3.72%MoM)，月增主因受惠歐美線價量齊揚。元富預估陽明
3Q26 營收 646.92 億元(+40.9%QoQ)。獲利方面，儘管中東衝突推升
燃油成本，且貨量成長帶動變動成本同步增加，惟 3Q26 運價仍維持
高 檔 ， 故 上 修 毛 利 率 至 31.54% ， 稅 後 淨 利 156.37 億元
(+172.7%QoQ)，3Q26 稅後 EPS 4.48 元。 
 2026 年供需缺口相對收斂，預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 8.17 元：展望
4Q26，需求面而言，歐線隨傳統旺季結束，終端需求動能轉弱，預期
運價將緩步回落；美線旺季需求亦近尾聲，惟受巴拿馬運河通行量下
降及中國十一長假前提前出貨支撐 ， 預期短期運價仍維持高原期。另，
9/24 川習會後關稅政策變化仍須持續關注。供給面而言，美伊衝突及
紅海通行風險仍存， 繞行好望角仍將限 制有效運力供給。 整體而言，
預估 2026/2027 年稅後 EPS 8.17/3.44 元。 
 買進轉中立，目標價 68 元：評價方面，以 2Q27F BVPS 計算目前 PB 
0.6X，介於陽明疫情前區間 0.5-1.7X 下緣。假設股利分配率 4 成，預
估發放現金股 3.3 元，目前殖利率 5.4%，惟傳統淡季將近，評等自買
進轉為中立，目標價 68 元(2Q27BVPS PB 0.7X)。

個股報告 
陽明為全球貨櫃運力第 9 大航
商，市占率 2.2% 
 陽明成立於 1972 年 12 月 ， 實收資本額349.21 億元 。 截至1H26， 全球共6,407
位員工及 233 個代理行 ， 每週提供105 條定期航線服務 。 根據Alphaliner 統計 ，
截至 Sep-26，陽明為全球貨櫃運力第 9 大航商，市佔率約 2.2%。自 Feb-25
起，陽明所屬聯盟由原先 THE Alliance 改組為 Premier Allianc e。陽明 1H26
航線運費收入占比別：亞洲近洋線 13%，亞二線(含中東、南美、紅海、澳洲、
南亞) 12%，歐洲線(含西北歐、地中海、越大西洋、歐地) 39%，美洲線(含美
東、美西) 36%。 
以船隊規模而言，截至 3Q26 陽明共有 99 艘船，包含自有船共 65 艘，租入船
34 艘，總運力 77.3 萬 TEU。為持續追求汰舊換新計畫，陽明預計 2026 年將
交付 5 艘 1.5 萬 TEU LNG 雙燃料船。

Sources: Company，MasterLink

2Q26 稅後 EPS 1.64 元，低於預
期 
 陽明 2Q26 營收 459.2 億元(+18.8%QoQ，+18.8%Y oY)， 季增主因歐美運價上
升。獲利方面，儘管油價上漲、內陸運輸燃油附加費增加，且歐美貨量占比提
升使變動成本上升，惟毛利率 17.5%、營益率 12.4%，皆優於原先預估。業外
方面，受到認列匯兌損失 0.36 億元影響，業外收益 12.91 億元，低於原先預
估，稅後淨利 57.3 億元(+299.4%QoQ，+482%Y oY)，2Q26 稅後 EPS 1.64
元，低於原先預估之 2 元。

3Q 26 美線運價優於預期 ，
預估稅後 EPS  4.48 元  
 根據上海航交所 9/18 公告，上海出口集裝箱運價指數(SCFI) 3,687.83 點
(+0.7%WoW，+207.78%Y oY)，遠東-美線皆續揚，漲幅維持低個位數，分別
為遠東-美西線+3.01%WoW，遠東-美東線+0.95%WoW；歐洲相關則續跌，遠
東-歐線-4.72%WoW，遠東-地中海-5.27%WoW。另，波斯灣航線為本次中東
衝突主要影響區域，本週運價轉跌，-1.03%WoW。

元富證券投資顧問股份有限公司 台北市敦化南路二段 97 號 19 樓 (02)2325-3299  (115)金管投顧新字第 016 號         Page 4 of 7 
個股報告 
陽明 Aug-26 營收 216.11 億元(+3.72%MoM，+56.17%Y oY，2026 年累計營
收+11.93%Y oY)，月增主因受惠歐美線價量齊揚。元富預估陽明 3Q26 營收
646.92 億元(+40.9%QoQ，+53.7%Y oY)。獲利方面，儘管中東衝突推升燃油
成本，且貨量成長帶動變動成本同步增加，惟 3Q26 運價仍維持高檔，故上修
毛利率至 31.54%、營益率 25.94%。假設匯率持穩，預估稅後淨利 156.37 億
元(+172.7%QoQ，+158.5%Y oY)，預估 3Q26 稅後 EPS 4.48 元。

Exhibit 9：近年新加坡海運燃油價格

Sources: Bloomberg(Sep-26)， Masterlink

2026 年供需缺口相對收斂， 預估
2026 年稅後 EPS 8.17 元 
 根據 Alphaliner Aug-26 預測，2026 年貨櫃供給年增 4.5%，需求年增 2.5%，
供給增幅仍高於需求，惟供需缺口較 2025 年逐步收斂。短期供給方面，目前
歐美港口以局部壅塞為主，如：9/23 歐洲鹿特丹港等待天數約 3 天、美國長灘
港約 6 天；中東地區則因地緣政治風險仍在，遠東-波斯灣航線運力供給受限，
支撐運價維持高檔；目前部分貨物改由印度西岸轉運後以陸路運往中東，使得
印度西岸港口壅塞，等待天數約 3-8 天。

展望 4Q26，需求面而言，歐線隨傳統旺季結束，終端需求動能轉弱，預期運
價將緩步回落；美線旺季需求亦近尾聲，惟受巴拿馬運河通行量下降及中國十
一長假前提前出貨支撐，預期短期運價仍維持高原期。另，9/24 川習會後關稅
政策變化仍須持續關注。供給面而言，美伊衝突及紅海通行風險仍存，儘管
Gemini 聯盟逐步恢復部分紅海航線，其餘航商短期跟進可能性仍低 ，繞行好
望角仍將限制有效運力供給。

整體而言，預估 2026 年營收 2,003.1 億元(+22.5%Y oY)，考量近期中東衝突
再度升溫，且歐美航線貨量占比提升帶動燃油成本增加，預估稅後淨利 285.1
億元(+66.8%Y oY)，2026 年稅後 EPS 8.17 元。展望 2027 年，考量新船陸續
交付 ， 供給壓力逐步增加 ， 且假設無新增關稅政策干擾 ， 需求回歸傳統季節性，
預估 2027 年稅後 EPS 3.44 元。

買進轉中立，目標價 68 元  評價方面 ， 以2Q27F BVPS 計算目前 PB 0.6X， 介於陽明疫情前區間0.5-1.7X
下緣。假設股利分配率 4 成，預估發放現金股 3.3 元，目前殖利率 5.4%，惟
傳統淡季將近，評等自買進轉為中立，目標價 68 元(2Q27BVPS PB 0.7X)。

風險提示  1) 燃油價格高於預期；2) 貨量及運價走勢不如預期；3) 匯率風險。

營收走勢圖

研究員/張書明 brady.chang@uni-psg.com

大量科技主要做PCB 機鑽與量測設備 ， 研究部維持強力買進投資建議 ，
係因 ： （1）總月產能已達 400 台並維持滿載 ， 預期今年出貨將超過1000
台，優於3 月法說展望；（2）TM 量測設備重要性與獨佔性不斷提升，
公司持續從 25 年 TM2 迭代至2H26 TM4 提升產品組合，預期拉貨比例
將從 8 台背鑽機對應 1 台 TM4 提升到 5：1，有望使公司市占率大幅
提升； （3）大量先進智慧在切入半導體大廠製程站點後，26-27 年也
將翻倍成長。綜上所述，預估26/27 年營收 97 與 137 億元上修到 125
與 229 億元（+146%/+83%YoY），稅後EPS 從 27 與 43 元上修到 28 與
70 元。研究部認為PCB 產業持續擴張，同時規格不斷升級。大量科技
位於客戶擴廠的風口浪尖，其量測技術與獨家產品成為公司提升市占
率與切入半導體的關鍵護城河，稼動率滿載的同時積極擴產。給予 27
年 22 倍 PE，目標價從 1220 元上修到 1540 元（2027 EPS*22PER）。

重點摘要： 
1. 公司簡介 
大量科技（3167）成立於 1980 年，總部位於桃園市八德區。
2004 年4 月興櫃，2013 年 10 月上市。主要提供 PCB 與半導體
設備，包含 PCB 標準鑽孔機、成型機與高階 CCD 背鑽機、CCD
成型機、TM 量測機，進行鑽孔定位、PCB 成型切割、鑽孔、塗
佈等；半導體設備包含 Metrology、AOI 與自動化設備即時量
測、檢測。台灣八德為總部，江蘇設有南京與漣水兩個工廠。
服務據點遍及中國、印度、東南亞及韓國、日本等地。主要客
戶為各大台系、中系 PCB 板廠， 競爭對手為德系 Schmoll（施
摩爾） 與中系大族數控。2025 年產品組合為 PCB 高階設備 
61%、低階設備 20% 與半導體 9%，25 年高低階出貨量約 4 : 
6。

2. 產品組合持續優化，2Q26 財報優於預期 
大量科技 2Q26 營收 28.6 億元 （+47%QoQ，+125%YoY） ，優於原
先預期 23.9 億元。毛利率 44.2%，優於原先預期 43.4%，顯
示高毛利產品佔比提升，產品組合持續優化。先前費用預估過
度樂觀，儘管費用從原先 2.73 億元調整至 4.97 億元（費用
率 17.4%），2Q26 稅後 EPS 仍達 6.10 元，優於原先預期。
2Q26 淨利率從 4 月 21% 一路月增至6 月達 33%， 稅後EPS 從單
月 1.55 元提升至 2.69 元。隨著訂單與出貨量持續墊加，公司
將逐月顯現營運槓桿帶來獲利大幅提升。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

3. 合約負債與在手訂單續創新高 
大量科技目前主力產品為 PCB CCD 高階背鑽機，隨著 AI 晶片
需求量與規格同步成長，近年背鑽機設備量與精度需求暴增，
大 量 科 技 高 階 設 備 出 貨 量 成 長 陡 峭 。25 年 出 貨 501 台
（+196%YoY），26 年截至 3M26 已出貨 139 台，26 年訂單能見
度達 800 台。依目前營收推估 26 年出貨應已超過 3 月預期，
且公司當時稼動率已逐漸滿載，設備交期已從 3 個月拉長到 9
個月。因此，研究部預估26 年出貨量成長性有望接近 25 年水
準 。 針對未來潛力產品TM 量測機系列 ，TM2 於 25 年首度推出 ，
出貨 27 台，1H26 以 TM3 大平台單軸機為主，未來則以 TM4 大
平台四軸機為主，2Q26 開始接單並於 3Q26 陸續出貨，帶動單
價與毛利提升。26 年截至 3M26 已出貨 7 台，當時預期全年出
貨將超過 70 台。

圖一、 3M26 在手訂單能見度

資料來源：公司、統一投顧整理

公司設備接單後通常先進入備料與組裝帶動存貨提升，部分客
戶下單時會支付 20-30% 訂金形成合約負債，部分客戶出貨才
付款。最終完成安裝、測試與客戶驗收後一次認列營收並收取
尾款。目前下單到出貨歷時約需 2-3 季，出貨到認列再歷時約
1 季。公司合約負債從25 年底 7.5 億元成長至 2Q26 15 億元，
保守假設合約負債約略反應未來 3 季 20% 的在手訂單 ， 目前在
手訂單將逾 75 億元，已超過25 年全年營收水準，顯示需求強
勁無虞。
